});\n\nfunction shiftLane(dir) {\n const next = G.lane + dir;\n if (next >= 0 && next < 3) {\n G.lane = next;\n G.targetX = LANES[next];\n }\n}\n\nfunction doJump() {\n if (!G.jumping) {\n G.jumping = \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nconst $score = document.getElementById('score');\nconst $hearts = document.querySelectorAll('#hearts span');\nconst $fill = document.getElementById('speed-fill');\nconst $final = document.getElementById('final-score');\nconst $best = document.getElementById('best-score');\n\nfunction refreshHUD() {\n $score.textContent = Math.floor(G.score);\n $hearts.forEach((h, i) => {\n h.style.opacity = i < G.lives ? '1' : '0.15';\n h.style.filter = i < G.lives ? '' : 'grayscale(1)';\n });\n const pct = Math.min(100, ((G.speed - 10) / 22) * 100);\n $fill.style.width = Math.max(4, pct) + '%';\n document.body.classList.toggle('fast', G.speed > 22);\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500\u2500 GAME EVENTS \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nfunction onHit() {\n G.hitCD = 1.4;\n G.lives--;\n refreshHUD();\n document.body.classList.remove('hit');\n void document.body.offsetWidth; // reflow to restart animation\n document.body.classList.add('hit');\n if (G.lives <= 0) setTimeout(onGameOver, 420);\n}\n\nfunction onGameOver() {\n G.running = false;\n const sc = Math.floor(G.score);\n if (sc > G.best) { G.best = document.getElementById('hud').classList.add('hidden');\n}\n\nfunction startGame() {\n Object.assign(G, {\n running: true, score: 0, lives: 3,\n lane: 1, targetX: LANES[1],\n speed: 10, camZ: 0, camY: prevT = 0;\n\nfunction loop(ts) {\n requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n\n const dt = Math.min((ts - prevT) / 1000, 0.05);\n prevT = ts;\n\n if (!G.running) {\n RENDERER.render(SCENE, CAM);\n return;\n }\n\n G.time += dt;\n\n // \u2500\u2500 Forward movement \u2500\u2500\n G.camZ -= G.speed * dt;\n CAM.position.z = G.camZ;\n\n // \u2500\u2500 Lane change (smooth lerp) \u2500\u2500\n CAM.position.x += (G.targetX - CAM.position.x) * Math.min(1, 13 * dt);\n\n // \u2500\u2500 Jump physics \u2500\u2500\n if (G.jumping) {\n G.velY += GRAVITY * dt;\n G.camY += G.velY * dt;\n if (G.camY <= EYE_H) {\n G.camY = EYE_H;\n G.velY = 0;\n G.jumping = false;\n }\n } else {\n // Running head-bob\n G.camY = EYE_H + Math.sin(G.time * 9) * 0.04;\n }\n CAM.position.y = G.camY;\n\n // \u2500\u2500 Recycle ground tiles \u2500\u2500\n tiles.forEach(t => {\n if (t.position.z > G.camZ + TILE_LEN) {\n t.position.z -= TILE_N * TILE_LEN;\n }\n });\n\n // \u2500\u2500 Spawn obstacles \u2500\u2500\n G.spawnT -= dt;\n if (G.spawnT <= 0) {\n // Occasionally spawn 2 lanes blocked (forces jump or tight dodge)\n if (Math.random() < 0.18 && G.time > 20) {\n const a = Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);\n const b = (a + 1) % 3;\n spawnTrain(a);\n spawnTrain(b);\n } else {\n spawnTrain();\n }\n G.spawnT = G.spawnR;\n G.spawnR = Math.max(0.82, G.spawnR - 0.022);\n }\n\n // \u2500\u2500 Obstacle update & collision \u2500\u2500\n G.hitCD -= dt;\n\n for (let i = obstacles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n const obs = obstacles[i];\n\n if (obs.position.z > G.camZ + DESPAWN_Z) {\n SCENE.remove(obs);\n obstacles.splice(i, 1);\n continue;\n }\n\n if (G.hitCD <= 0) {\n const dx = Math.abs(obs.position.x - CAM.position.x);\n const dz = Math.abs(obs.position.z - G.camZ);\n // Allow jumping over (train top \u2248 obs.y+1.1 = 2.2; max jump \u2248 2.9)\n const aboveTrain = CAM.position.y > obs.position.y + 1.3;\n if (dx < 1.05 && dz < 2.3 && !aboveTrain) {\n SCENE.remove(obs);\n obstacles.splice(i, 1);\n onHit();\n }\n }\n }\n\n // \u2500\u2500 Score & speed \u2500\u2500\n G.score += G.speed * dt;\n G.speed = Math.min(32, 10 + G.time * 0.38);\n\n // \u2500\u2500 Neon lights follow player \u2500\u2500\n neonL.position.set(G.targetX - 4, 2.5, G.camZ - 9);\n neonR.position.set(G.targetX + 4, 2.5, G.camZ - 9);\n\n refreshHUD();\n RENDERER.render(SCENE, CAM);\n}\n\n// \u2500\u2500\u2500 RESIZE \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nwindow.addEventListener('resize', () => {\n CAM.aspect = innerWidth / innerHeight;\n CAM.updateProjectionMatrix();\n RENDERER.setSize(innerWidth, innerHeight);\n});\n\n// \u2500\u2500\u2500 BOOT Esquiva los trenes en una ciudad nocturna. ## Controles - ⬅️ ➡️ Cambiar carril | ⬆️ ESPACIO — Saltar - 📱 Swipe en móvil ## Deploy - GitHub Pages: Settings → Pages → main branch - app.emergent.sh → Import from GitHub `; function toB64(str) { // UTF-8 safe base64 encode return btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent(str))); } function log(msg, type='info') { const d = document.getElementById('log'); d.style.display = 'block'; const el = document.createElement('div'); el.className = 'log-item ' + type; el.textContent = msg; d.appendChild(el); el.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth'}); } async function ghRequest(method, path, body, token) { const res = await fetch('https://api.github.com' + path, { method, headers: { 'Authorization': 'token ' + token, 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Accept': 'application/vnd.github.v3+json', }, body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined, }); const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({})); return { ok: res.ok, status: res.status, data }; } async function uploadFile(username, repo, path, content, token, isBinary=false) { const b64 = isBinary ? content : toB64(content); // Check if file exists (need SHA to update) const check = await ghRequest('GET', `/repos/${username}/${repo}/contents/${path}`, null, token); const sha = check.ok ? check.data.sha : undefined; const r = await ghRequest('PUT', `/repos/${username}/${repo}/contents/${path}`, { message: `Add ${path}`, content: b64, ...(sha ? {sha} : {}) }, token); return r; } async function pushAll() { const username = document.getElementById('username').value.trim(); const token = document.getElementById('token').value.trim(); const repo = document.getElementById('reponame').value.trim(); if (!username || !token || !repo) { alert('Rellena todos los campos primero.'); return; } document.getElementById('pushBtn').disabled = true; document.getElementById('log').innerHTML = ''; document.getElementById('log').style.display = 'block'; document.getElementById('result').style.display = 'none'; try { // 1. Create repo log('⏳ Creando repositorio…'); const cr = await ghRequest('POST', '/user/repos', { name: repo, description: 'First-person endless runner con Three.js 🚂', private: false, auto_init: false, }, token); if (cr.ok) { log('✅ Repositorio creado: ' + cr.data.full_name, 'ok'); } else if (cr.status === 422) { log('ℹ️ Repositorio ya existe, actualizando archivos…', 'info'); } else { throw new Error(`No se pudo crear el repo (${cr.status}): ${cr.data.message}`); } // 2. Upload files const files = [ { path:'index.html', content: FILE_HTML }, { path:'style.css', content: FILE_CSS }, { path:'game.js', content: FILE_JS }, { path:'player.jpg', content: PLAYER_B64, binary: true }, { path:'README.md', content: README }, ]; for (const f of files) { log(`⏳ Subiendo ${f.path}…`); const r = await uploadFile(username, repo, f.path, f.content, token, f.binary || false); if (r.ok) { log(`✅ ${f.path} subido`, 'ok'); } else { throw new Error(`Error subiendo ${f.path}: ${r.data.message}`); } } // 3. Enable GitHub Pages log('⏳ Activando GitHub Pages…'); const pr = await ghRequest('POST', `/repos/${username}/${repo}/pages`, { source: { branch: 'main', path: '/' } }, token); if (pr.ok || pr.status === 409) { log('✅ GitHub Pages activado', 'ok'); } else { log('ℹ️ GitHub Pages: actívalo manualmente en Settings → Pages', 'info'); } // Done! const repoUrl = `https://github.com/${username}/${repo}`; const pagesUrl = `https://${username}.github.io/${repo}`; document.getElementById('repoLink').href = repoUrl; document.getElementById('pagesLink').href = pagesUrl; document.getElementById('pagesLink').textContent = '🌐 ' + pagesUrl; document.getElementById('result').style.display = 'block'; log('🎉 ¡Todo listo!', 'ok'); } catch(err) { log('❌ ' + err.message, 'err'); document.getElementById('pushBtn').disabled = false; } }
